This vindicates associationist ideals of a uniform, gradual, error-driven learning mechanism operating across domains, and defuses the once-influential argument that associationist mechanisms are too limited to account for human cognitive capacities. At the same time, the successes of deep learning depend on computational architectures that go well beyond anything classical associationists envisaged, and supervised learning operates within these as one component rather than a complete account of learning.

</details>


### [186] [Specifying AI-SDLC Processes: A Protocol Language for Human-Agent Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20615)
*Ylli Prifti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于AI-SDLC流程的领域特定语言（DSL），通过形式化语法、良构条件、操作语义和执行不变量，区分策略（声明意图）与机制（结构强制），从而约束流程非确定性。实验表明结构强制比行为合规更能有效控制系统故障率，并形式化了2+N团队模式（2个人类控制角色+N个智能体成员）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体参与软件开发生命周期时，缺乏专门的语言来表达人机责任边界、审批关卡和治理约束。现有方法要么将流程编码在智能体提示中（易漂移），要么针对相邻领域（工作流管理、业务流程），或仅处理碎片化问题（访问控制、审批关卡）。

Method: 提出一种领域特定语言（DSL），包含形式化语法、良构条件、操作语义和执行不变量。核心创新是区分策略（声明意图）与机制（结构强制），通过验证令牌和能力边界等原语来约束流程非确定性。形式化了2+N团队模式（2个人类控制角色+N个智能体成员）。

Result: 故障率分析表明：结构强制将系统故障率限制在智能体和验证器权重的乘积范围内，而行为合规会导致累积或接近饱和的故障增长。2+N团队模式形式化了AI-SDLC中的经典职责分离原则。克莱尼闭包编排循环和自反协议遵守验证成为设计属性而非特例构造。

Conclusion: 与MetaGPT、FlowAgent、BPMN扩展和基于能力的SAGA安全方案对比，本工作的新颖性在于特定的集成方式而非单一原语。已有可行实现验证了可行性，实证评估留待未来工作。

Abstract: AI agents now participate as first-class team members across the software development lifecycle, yet no specification language exists for expressing the human-agent responsibility boundaries, approval gates, and governance constraints this collaboration requires. Existing approaches encode process in agent prompts (subject to drift), target adjacent domains (workflow management, business processes), or address only fragments (access control, approval gates).
  We propose a domain-specific language for specifying AI-SDLC processes as protocols, with formal syntax, well-formedness conditions, operational semantics, and enforcement invariants. The language distinguishes policy (declared intent) from mechanism (structural enforcement), enabling implementations to bound process non-determinism through primitives such as validation tokens and capability boundaries.
  Three results follow. A failure rate analysis shows that structural enforcement bounds system failure rates at a weighted product of agent and validator rates, while behavioral compliance permits cumulative or near-saturating growth. The 2+N team pattern (two human-in-control roles plus N specialized agent members) formalizes classical Separation of Duties for AI-SDLC. Kleene closure of orchestration loops and reflexive protocol-adherence validation emerge as design properties rather than special-case constructs.
  We position the contribution against multi-agent frameworks (MetaGPT), workflow specification (FlowAgent, BPMN extensions), and capability-based security (SAGA): the novelty lies in the specific integration, not any single primitive. A working implementation demonstrates feasibility; empirical evaluation is future work.

</details>


### [187] [PEAR: Permutation-Equivariant Adaptive Routing Multi-Agent Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20621)
*Yang Feng,Ziwei Xu,Xia Hu,Fengxiang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PEAR是一种排列等变的自适应路由多智能体辩论协议，通过动态重新配置通信角色和稀疏拓扑来提升LLM推理可靠性，在四个基准和六个模型上超越现有辩论方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有固定拓扑的多智能体辩论存在持续的位置偏差、不可靠智能体放大以及角色分配敏感等问题，需要一种动态且公平的通信机制来提升辩论效果和模型可靠性。

Method: 提出排列等变自适应路由多智能体辩论（PEAR）协议，在连续辩论轮次中动态切换智能体到角色的分配，并重新配置稀疏拓扑结构，实现基于智能体状态的动态路由。

Result: 在四个推理基准和六个不同LLM骨干上的实验表明，PEAR的平均准确率显著优于最强的辩论基线方法。

Conclusion: PEAR作为一种等变稀疏路由器，能在保证准确率的前提下降低路由复杂度并提升泛化能力，有效解决了固定拓扑多智能体辩论中的不公平性和偏差问题。

Abstract: Multi-agent debate improves the reliability of large language models (LLMs) through iterative peer critiques. However, fixed topologies often introduce persistent positional biases, amplify unreliable agents, and cause high sensitivity to role assignments. We introduce \textit{Permutation-Equivariant Adaptive Routing Multi-Agent Debate (PEAR)}, an inference-time protocol that dynamically reconfigures communication roles and sparse topologies across consecutive debate rounds. By strategically switching agent-to-role assignments based on evolving agent states, PEAR prevents any agent from permanently occupying a privileged network position or distributes influence more evenly across the debate. We theoretically characterize PEAR as an equivariant sparse router: it preserves accuracy under agent relabeling while reducing routing complexity and improving generalization. Comprehensive empirical evaluations across four reasoning benchmarks and six diverse LLM backbones demonstrate PEAR significantly improves average accuracy over the strongest debate baselines. The code is at https://github.com/EVIEHub/PEAR.

</details>


### [188] [Darwin Mobile Agent: A Roadmap for Self-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20622)
*Daniel Beechey,Derek Yuen,Jianheng Liu,Dezhao Luo,Tiantian He,Weilin Luo,Jun Wang,Kun Shao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出通过移动GUI作为复杂世界的代理，构建Darwin Mobile Agent框架，利用异步云手机环境实现自主强化学习，并规划了逐步去除人类先验的路线图，最终目标实现自演化GUI代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI智能体依赖大量人类先验知识，限制了其在开放环境中涌现通用、自适应行为的能力。作者希望去除人类先验，让智能体通过与极其复杂的"大世界"交互自然演化。

Method: 提出Darwin Mobile Agent框架，使用异步代理-环境循环在并行云手机实例上运行，解决真实移动交互中数据收集瓶颈问题。同时提出从任务课程、结果验证和记忆管理三个维度系统去除人类先验的概念路线图。

Result: 验证了Darwin基础设施在GUI领域策略优化所需的稳定性和可扩展性，为自主自演化GUI代理奠定了实践和理论基础。

Conclusion: 移动GUI是实现通用人工智能的有效代理环境，通过Darwin框架和去人类先验路线图，可以迈向真正自主、自演化的GUI代理。

Abstract: The goal of artificial intelligence is to create agents capable of general, adaptive behaviour in open-ended environments. Guided by the "Bitter Lesson", we argue that the most effective path toward this goal is to systematically remove human priors and allow intelligence to naturally emerge through interaction with a "Big World" that is orders of magnitude more complex than the agent itself. We propose the mobile Graphical User Interface (GUI) as a practical proxy for such a world and introduce Darwin Mobile Agent, an open-source infrastructure designed as a foundation for autonomous reinforcement learning in this domain. This framework addresses the data-collection bottleneck in real-world mobile interactions by using an asynchronous agent-environment loop across parallel cloud-phone instances. We further propose a conceptual roadmap to systematically remove human priors from three fundamental pillars of a self-evolving agent: task curricula, outcome verification, and memory management. We validate that the Darwin infrastructure provides the stability and scalability required for the first stage of this roadmap: policy optimisation in the GUI domain. This work establishes the practical and theoretical foundation necessary to move toward truly autonomous, self-evolving GUI agents.

</details>


### [189] [Path-dependent program induction under resource constraints explains human sequence learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20623)
*Hanqi Zhou,David G. Nagy,Peter Dayan,Charley M. Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文整合率失真理论与程序归纳，提出层级适应文法（HAG）模型，在有限认知资源下解释人类如何从序列经验中构建抽象可重用知识。模拟实验表明HAG优于固定文法，在线旋律学习实验中，层级库模型最好地解释了个体差异与泛化行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在有限认知资源约束下，人类如何从序列化经验中构建抽象、可复用的知识结构。

Method: 将率失真理论与程序归纳相结合，提出层级适应文法（HAG）模型，包含局部（任务内）和全局（任务间）两种知识库，受记忆与计算约束共同调控。

Result: 模拟中HAG取得了更优的率失真权衡和更强的泛化能力；在线旋律学习实验中，参与者的回忆错误呈现系统简化模式，层级库模型最好地解释了个体差异与续写选择。

Conclusion: 结构化学习可视为有界程序归纳过程，经验的先后顺序塑造了学习者未来构建的抽象知识。

Abstract: How do people build abstract, reusable knowledge from sequential experience under bounded cognitive resources? To answer this question, we integrate rate-distortion theory with recent advances in program induction to describe how prior knowledge shapes which future structures are cheap to encode and easy to discover. We formalize this in a hierarchical Adaptor Grammar (HAG) with distinct local (within-task) and global (across-task) libraries, governed jointly by constraints on memory and computation. In simulations, HAG achieves better rate-distortion trade-offs and stronger generalization than fixed grammars or shallow chunking methods. In an online melodic sequence-learning experiment, participants' recall errors reflected systematic simplifications and reaction times increased at inferred program boundaries. Trial-by-trial fits further showed that hierarchical libraries best explained individual differences in both recall and out-of-sample continuation choices, outperforming all alternative models. These findings cast structured learning as bounded program induction in which the order of experience shapes future abstractions a learner builds.

</details>


### [190] [In LLM Reasoning, there is Irrationality on top of Value Misalignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20624)
*Kejiang Qian,Fengxiang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并形式化了“理性价值风险”概念，指即使LLM经过良好对齐训练，在推理时仍可能无法最大化对齐价值。实验表明该风险普遍存在，对齐训练可降低但无法消除，且对推理策略高度敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐工作主要关注训练阶段的价值对齐，但忽略了推理阶段模型实际使用的推理策略与理论上最优策略之间的效用差距，作者试图从理论上量化并实证验证这一差距。

Method: 数学形式化理性价值风险为部署推理策略与理性策略（最陡方向上期望效用最大化的响应）之间的效用差异，并将其估计误差分解为有限候选、有限提示和不完美验证器三部分，在多个模型家族和基准上进行了大规模实验。

Result: (1)理性价值风险普遍存在；(2)价值对齐可降低但无法消除该风险；(3)该风险对推理时策略高度敏感；(4)更长推理可提升理性但边际收益递减。

Conclusion: 对齐后的LLM在推理时仍存在价值最大化不足的问题，需要关注推理策略优化以弥合理性与实际表现之间的差距。

Abstract: Significant progress has been made in aligning LLMs with target value functions. We argue that, even when an LLM has been well aligned in (post-)training, it may still fail to maximise the aligned value in reasoning. We mathematically formalise this gap as rational value risk: the utility discrepancy between a model's deployed reasoning strategy and its rational counterpart, which is defined to be the responses that maximise expected utility in the steepest direction. The estimation error of rational value risk is further decomposed into three components from finite candidates, finite prompts, and imperfect verifiers. Extensive experiments are conducted, covering models Llama-3.1, Qwen-2.5, T{\"}ulu-3 families (7B-72B), GPT-5.2, GPT-5.5, and DeepSeek-V4, and benchmarks UltraFeedback, AlpacaEval, GSM8K, MATH, HumanEval, and MathArena. The results validate that (1) rational value risk is widespread; (2) value alignment can reduce, but cannot eliminate, it; (3) the risk is highly sensitive to inference-time reasoning strategy; and (4) longer reasoning improves rationality with diminishing returns. The code is at https://github.com/EVIEHub/LLM-Rationality.

</details>


### [191] [AlphaMemo: Structured Search-Process Memory for Self-Evolving Alpha Mining Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20625)
*Hang Yu,Zifan Zheng,Jeff Z. Pan,Tongliang Liu,Zhiyong Wang,Fengxiang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AlphaMemo，一种带结构化搜索-过程记忆的自进化因子挖掘智能体，通过记录AST差异中的编辑模式、置信门控残差记忆和非对称否决控制，提升Alpha挖掘的效率和稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在挖掘Alpha因子时面临组合搜索空间大、反馈噪声非平稳、发现冗余以及过度拟合等问题。

Method: 1) 从AST差异中提取编辑模式；2) 在搜索账本先验上构建置信门控残差记忆；3) 使用非对称否决控制抑制高置信度失败模式。

Result: 在CSI 500和S&P 500数据集上，AlphaMemo在样本外表现和固定预算下的发现效率均有提升。

Conclusion: 残差学习、置信门控、AST差异模式和非对称否决控制等设计均有效，代码已开源。

Abstract: LLM agents are promising for alpha mining via combining financial priors, symbolic reasoning, executable factor generation, and feedback-driven refinement. Yet, they face a combinatorial search space, noisy non-stationary feedback, redundant discoveries, and overfitting risks from naively reusing past successes. To address these challenges, we propose AlphaMemo, a self-evolving alpha mining agent with Structured Search-Process Memory. Rather than memorizing only final factors or full trajectories, AlphaMemo records reusable evidence about which edit motifs work or fail under specific parent-factor contexts. It extracts motifs from Abstract Syntax Tree (AST) differences, applies confidence-gated residual memory on top of a search-ledger prior, and uses asymmetric veto control to suppress high-confidence failure patterns. Experiments on CSI 500 and S\&P 500 show improved out-of-sample performance and fixed-budget discovery efficiency, with ablations validating the roles of residual learning, confidence gating, AST-diff motifs, and veto memory. Code is at https://github.com/jarrettyu/AlphaMemo.

</details>


### [192] [Latent Goal Prediction from Language for Model-Based Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20627)
*Samuel Barbeau,Simon Roy,Giovanni Beltrame,Christian Desrosiers,Nicolas Thome*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LAGO框架，通过在潜在空间中同时预测语言指令分解的中间目标状态和动作条件轨迹，解决了世界模型规划中的累积预测误差和语言-视觉对齐问题，实现了长视野下稳健精确的语言引导规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型规划方法面临两大瓶颈：视觉目标提供精确局部梯度但缺乏远距离引导，语言目标灵活但受限于跨模态对齐噪声或依赖不适合高频采样的大模型。

Method: LAGO在统一潜在空间中同时预测（1）从语言指令分解出的中间目标状态序列和（2）动作条件 rollout；通过在线更新子目标并使用软最小轨迹成本进行规划。

Result: 在多个环境和规划视野下，LAGO避免了先前方法的性能急剧退化，实现了稳健精确的长视野规划。

Conclusion: LAGO桥接了视觉目标的精确性与语言引导的灵活性，实现了纯语言驱动的长视野规划。

Abstract: Planning with world models is bottlenecked by compounding prediction errors and the difficulty of defining optimizable goals. Visual targets provide precise local gradients but poor distant guidance, while language is flexible yet limited by noisy cross-modal alignment or dependence on large generative models unsuited for the high-sampling nature of model-based planning. To address these challenges, we introduce Latent Goal Prediction from Language (LAGO), a framework that predicts both sequences of intermediate goal states from language instructions and action-conditioned rollouts, all within the same latent space. Rather than optimizing toward a single global objective, LAGO dynamically decomposes instructions into explicitly predicted, locally tractable latent subgoals. By updating these subgoals online and using a soft minimum trajectory cost during planning, LAGO enables an agent to follow coherent latent trajectories over long horizons. Evaluation across multiple environments planning horizons shows that LAGO avoids the sharp degradation of prior methods. By achieving robust and precise long-horizon planning purely from language, LAGO bridges the precision of visual goals with the flexibility of text-guided control.

</details>


### [193] [Human Decision-Making with AI Assistance under Correlated Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20628)
*Yanru Guan,Naveen Raman,Fei Fang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究在特征相关情况下，AI应如何推荐测试以平衡短期决策质量和长期人类学习，证明最优策略需遵循"先探索后承诺"结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当不同特征（如测试结果）存在相关性时，AI应推荐哪些测试以平衡短期决策质量和长期人类学习，这是一个自然但尚未解决的问题。

Method: 理论证明（最优策略的探索-承诺结构、NP-hard性）、动态规划算法（有限时域最优策略）、近似算法（短时域规划+平稳后缀）。

Result: 特征相关性导致平稳策略性能极差；最优策略必须遵循探索-承诺结构；探索长度依赖于特征相关程度；计算最优策略是NP难的。

Conclusion: 特征相关性越强，探索阶段应越长；所提近似算法能实现接近最优的性能。

Abstract: Humans increasingly make decisions with AI assistance; for example, doctors may follow AI-recommended diagnostic tests and base their diagnoses on the results. A natural question is which tests should AI recommend to balance short-term decision quality and long-term human learning when different features (e.g., test results) are correlated. While prior work establishes that stationary policies that recommend the same tests repeatedly are optimal when features are independent, we prove that feature correlations lead such policies to perform arbitrarily poorly. Instead, we prove that any optimal policy must follow an explore-then-commit structure; initially, the AI should offer diverse tests so humans can learn accurate feature coefficients, then the AI should commit to a single set of tests, with exploration length that depends on the degree of feature correlation. We prove that computing the optimal policy is NP-hard and derive a dynamic programming-based algorithm that finds the optimal policy for finite horizons. We additionally develop an approximation that plans for shorter horizons and appends a stationary suffix, achieving near-optimal performance. Our empirical results complement our theory by showing that stronger feature correlation leads to longer exploration phases.

</details>


### [194] [Harnessing Agent Skills: Architectural Patterns and a Reference Architecture for Skill-Mediated LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20631)
*Boming Xia,Liming Zhu,Zhenchang Xing,Qinghua Lu,Dino Sejdinovic,Xiwei Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于LLM智能体的技能利用参考架构，包含10种架构模式（5个核心+5个辅助），分为供应链、中介、执行控制、证据与反馈四个责任层，并通过8个系统进行了交叉实例化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体技能在静态时是工件，但在运行时被选择、绑定上下文、由随机智能体解释并记录执行证据，这种运行时的特定关系称为"技能使用中"。需要系统性地研究从技能工件到技能使用中的架构职责。

Method: 通过实证研究提炼10种架构模式，将其综合为四层参考架构（供应链层、中介层、执行控制层、证据与反馈层），并在8个系统中进行交叉实例化评估。

Result: 提炼出10种经验驱动的架构模式（5核心+5辅助），构建了四层参考架构，并在8个系统中验证了其实例化可行性。

Conclusion: 所提出的模式和参考架构为分析不同智能体系统中的技能利用职责提供了统一的词汇和诊断框架。

Abstract: Agent skills externalise reusable agent-facing behavioural knowledge and guidance as persistent artefacts that can be discovered, activated, and interpreted by LLM agents. Although a skill artefact is static at rest, its architectural responsibilities arise in use, when the artefact is selected for a run, bound to context and authority constraints, interpreted by a stochastic agent, and recorded as run evidence. We call this run-specific relation skill-in-use. This paper studies agent skill harnessing: the architectural responsibilities that govern the transition from skill artefacts to skill-in-use, bound the executable consequences associated with skill-in-use, and capture evidence for attribution, verification, repair, and evolution. This paper provides a catalogue of ten empirically grounded architectural patterns (five core, five supporting) for skill harnessing and synthesises them into a reference architecture with four responsibility layers: Supply Chain, Mediation, Execution Control, and Evidence & Feedback. We evaluate the architecture through cross-instantiation across 8 selected systems. The resulting patterns and reference architecture provide a vocabulary and diagnostic frame for analysing skill-harnessing responsibilities across agent systems.

</details>


### [195] [DEMM-Bench: A Cross-Regime Benchmark for Agent-Runtime Governance-Evidence Sufficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20634)
*Oleg Solozobov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DEMM-Bench基准，用于评估智能体运行时系统在八个证据体制下的决策治理证据充分性，发现现有方法在75%案例中存在过度声明，而提出的方法在零过度声明下达到56.25%的属性充分性准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体运行时系统虽然能生成多种记录（如追踪、账本、策略日志等），但这些记录并不一定能回答关于特定决策的治理问题，缺乏跨体制评估决策证据充分性的统一基准。

Method: 基于决策证据成熟度模型(DEMM)，通过适配器标准化八种证据体制，对行为者、权威、行动、策略、决策依据、资源接触、生命周期上下文和验证强度等属性提问，并应用八种确定性降级条件进行评估。

Result: 在64个手稿案例中，trace-present和schema-present基线在75%案例中过度声明，ledger-present在50%中过度声明，而提出的删节属性级候选评分器零过度声明，平均属性充分性准确率为56.25%。

Conclusion: DEMM-Bench为异构智能体运行时证据基质的决策证据成熟度提供了可重复评估的基准，并公开了数据集、构建预言标签、基线和适配器。

Abstract: Agent-runtime systems emit traces, ledgers, provenance graphs, policy logs, delegation tokens, cache events, and tool-firewall records, but those containers do not necessarily answer governance questions about a specific decision. DEMM-Bench is a cross-regime benchmark for agent-runtime governance-evidence sufficiency, grounded in the Decision Evidence Maturity Model (DEMM): it measures whether records across eight evidence regimes are sufficient to reconstruct decision-level properties rather than merely present. The benchmark normalizes the regimes through adapters, asks property questions over actor, authority, action, policy, decision basis, resource touch, lifecycle context, and verification strength, and applies eight deterministic degradation conditions. Across 64 manuscript cases, trace-present and schema-present baselines overclaim on 75% of cases, ledger-present overclaims on 50%, and the redacted property-level candidate scorer has zero overclaim with 56.25% mean Property Sufficiency Accuracy. The deposited package provides the 64-case dataset, construction-oracle labels, baselines, and adapters, supporting reproducible evaluation of decision-evidence maturity across heterogeneous agent-runtime evidence substrates.

</details>


### [196] [SkillHarness: Harnessing Safe Skills for Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20636)
*Yurun Chen,Biao Yi,Keting Yin,Shengyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SkillHarness框架，通过安全约束和选择性技能复用，使计算机使用智能体在动态环境中安全学习和使用技能，将不安全率降低57.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能学习方法假设环境是静态安全的，忽视了对抗交互（如提示注入）和环境动态变化（如弹窗）带来的风险，导致技能学习不安全且执行脆弱，亟需一种能够在动态环境中安全学习和使用技能的方法。

Method: SkillHarness框架包含两个核心模块：(1) 技能边界（Skill Boundary），利用多源监督信号从交互轨迹中识别安全技能，并在技能生命周期中构建自改进的安全约束；(2) 选择性技能复用（Selective Skill Reuse），引导任务根据上下文进行分解，并通过选择性激活技能子集完成任务。

Result: 在实验中，SkillHarness将所学技能的不安全率降低了57.1%，并且在动态环境变化下持续提升了执行稳定性，优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过将技能学习与利用建模为安全约束的交互过程，SkillHarness有效解决了动态环境中计算机使用智能体的安全技能学习问题，为智能体在现实动态环境中的可靠部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Computer-Use Agents (CUAs) are increasingly deployed in dynamic interactive environments, creating a growing need for continual skill learning during interaction. Recent approaches address this challenge by learning reusable skills from successful trajectories. However, these skill learning methods largely assume static and safe environments, overlooking risks from adversarial interactions (e.g., prompt injections) and environmental dynamics (e.g., pop-ups). In dynamic settings, such assumptions can lead to risky skill learning and brittle execution, undermining the reliability of CUAs. This raises the question: how can CUAs learn and use skills safely in dynamic environments? To address this problem, we propose SkillHarness, a framework for safe skill harnessing in dynamic environments. SkillHarness moves beyond static skill abstractions by modeling skill learning and utilization as a safety-constrained interaction process. Specifically, we introduce the skill boundary that leverages multi-source supervision signals to identify safe skills from interaction trajectories, and construct self-improving safety constraints throughout the skill lifecycle. In addition, SkillHarness introduces selective skill reuse, where tasks are guided to decompose according to context and completed through the selective activation of skill subsets. Our experiments demonstrate that SkillHarness significantly reduces the unsafe rate of learned skills by 57.1% and consistently improves execution stability under dynamic environmental changes, outperforming existing baselines.

</details>


### [197] [Constituency Optimisation Through Hamiltonian Representation Of Mandates (COTHROM): Algorithmic Redistricting of Irish Election Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20637)
*Ruaidhrí Campion,Matthew Fenlon,Joshua Cooney Mercedal,Casey Farren-Colloty,Eliza Somerville,Michael A. J. Mitchell*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次提出基于统计物理和MCMC方法的爱尔兰PR-STV选举重新划分选区框架，通过Potts哈密顿量优化多目标宪法要求，在科克郡的实验中优于现有法律边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 爱尔兰PR-STV选举重新划分选区面临巨大搜索空间中寻找最优代表性边界的问题，且"代表性"涉及多个相互冲突的宪法目标，缺乏系统性的优化框架。

Method: 将选区重划问题解析为统计物理中的Potts哈密顿量，宪法目标作为能量项；采用MCMC和模拟退火最小化目标函数，耦合常数作为目标权重代理；结合多准则决策分析和帕累托最优性解决权重选择模糊性。

Result: 在科克郡的比例代表性和紧凑性目标评估中，COTHRM框架在多种目标权重组合下均一致优于现有的法定选区边界。

Conclusion: 该计算框架能系统性地优化多宪法要求，量化权衡关系，为爱尔兰选举重新划分选区提供有效的决策支持工具。

Abstract: Electoral redistricting in Ireland's Proportional Representation Single Transferable Vote (PR-STV) system faces the challenge of selecting an optimally representative set of electoral boundaries from an enormous set of possible configurations, and where ``representative'' is a delicate balance of constitutional objectives that are often in tension with one another. We present the first computational framework for Irish electoral redistricting that systematically optimises across multiple constitutional requirements while making trade-offs explicit and quantifiable. The electoral redistricting problem is parsed using statistical physics, where constitutional objectives are considered as terms in a Potts Hamiltonian. Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) methods and simulated annealing are employed to minimise this objective function, systematically exploring this configuration space, with coupling constants as proxies for objective weightings. Multi Criterion Decision Analysis (MCDA) and Pareto Optimality is then utilised to remedy the ambiguity in choosing a certain objective weighting combination over others. With respect to proportional representation and compactness objectives evaluated in County Cork, COTHROM consistently improves on the existing legal constituency boundaries for a range of objective weightings.

</details>


### [198] [RIZZ: Routing Interactions to Near Zero-Interference Zones for Continual Adaptation of Black-Box Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20638)
*Sonali Goel,Pranav Vaidhyanathan,Lucas Schorling,Natalia Ares,Maike Osborne*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RIZZ是一个通过验证器门控记忆、路由和提示编译实现的大型语言模型持续适应框架，能在不访问模型权重的情况下，通过自然语言反馈进行在线黑盒适应，并显式控制干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒适应方法在处理非平稳输入流、稀疏反馈以及不同任务族间的行为污染时表现不佳，亟需一种能够在线适应且显式控制干扰的持续学习框架。

Method: 提出RIZZ框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 动态生成的内存分支组织输入流；(2) 上下文感知路由器选择或创建分支，检索分支本地/全局/图结构/工作记忆并编译为有界提示；(3) 任务验证器对输出评分，仅已验证的交互可更新记忆、提升/降级规则或创建反模式。

Result: 在多个竞争性基准测试上，RIZZ框架优于现有的最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: RIZZ为复合语言模型系统提供了一种有效的持续适应方案，能够在上下文预算限制下进行在线学习，同时通过显式干扰控制避免任务间污染。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed as long-lived agents that must adapt across users, tasks, domains, modalities, and feedback regimes without access to model weights. Existing black-box adaptation methods typically optimize a single prompt, maintain an undifferentiated memory, or rely on repeated rollout-heavy search. However, these designs struggle when streams of input are nonstationary, feedback is sparse, and failures from one task family can contaminate behavior on another. We introduce RIZZ (Routing Interactions to Near Zero-interference Zones), a continual adaptation framework for compound language-model systems that learns entirely through verifier-gated memory, routing, and prompt compilation. RIZZ organizes input streams into dynamically spawned memory branches. At inference time, either while online or offline, a context-aware router selects or creates a branch that retrieves branch-local, global, graph-structured, and working-memory context, which is compiled into a bounded prompt together with retrieved task evidence. After the model acts, task verifiers score the output, and only verified interactions can update memory, promote reusable rules, demote harmful rules, or create anti-patterns. This yields a black-box agent that improves through persistent natural-language feedback while explicitly controlling interference. RIZZ targets the regime where adaptation must occur online under context budgets. Finally, we demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework against state-of-the-art baselines on competitive benchmarks.

</details>


### [199] [An LLM-Explainable DRL Framework for Passenger-Directed Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20640)
*Ouided Braoui,Meriem Bouali,Nadir Farhi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将深度强化学习（DRL）与大型语言模型（LLM）相结合，用于自动驾驶车辆的决策控制与行为解释，通过在模拟中训练Dueling Double DQN agent实现三种驾驶模式，并利用LLM模块生成安全导向的解释，证明了该框架在安全性、适应性和可解释性方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆虽然具有更安全、更高效出行的潜力，但由于其决策过程缺乏透明度，公众信任度有限。本文旨在解决这一可解释性不足的问题。

Method: 结合深度强化学习（DRL）进行自适应驾驶控制，使用Dueling Double Deep Q-Network训练agent实现快速、舒适、停止三种驾驶模式切换；同时集成基于大语言模型（LLM）的可解释性模块，用于解读乘客请求、判断何时需要解释，并生成简洁的安全导向解释。

Result: DRL agent在模拟中展示了稳定的学习能力、安全遵守交通规则，以及单次行程中可靠的模式切换；LLM模块在请求延迟或因安全约束被覆盖时效果最佳。该框架平衡了安全性、适应性和可解释性。

Conclusion: 该工作作为将强化学习决策与LLM解释集成的概念验证，证明了在自动驾驶中结合RL决策与LLM可解释性的可行性，尤其在安全约束导致乘客请求被延迟或覆盖时，系统的可解释性最为有效。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles offer the potential for safer and more efficient mobility, yet public trust remains limited due to the lack of transparency in their decision-making. This work addresses this issue by combining deep reinforcement learning (DRL) for adaptive driving control with large language model (LLM)-based explainability modules designed to communicate agent behavior to passengers. DRL agents were trained in simulation using a Dueling Double Deep Q-Network to follow distinct driving requests: \textit{fast}, \textit{comfort}, and \textit{stop}. They demonstrated stable learning, safe compliance with traffic rules, and reliable switching between modes within a single trip. In parallel, LLM modules were introduced to interpret passenger requests, determine when explanations were needed, and generate concise, safety-oriented justifications. Results show that this framework, serving as a proof of concept for integrating RL decision-making and LLMs, balances safety, adaptability, and explainability, and is most effective when requests are delayed or overridden due to safety constraints.

</details>


### [200] [Hypothesis-Disciplined Multi-Agent Automated Formalization of Asymptotic Statistical Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20642)
*Tingzhou Wei,Zeyu Zheng,Ethan X. Fang,Junwei Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种假设驱动的Lean 4形式化验证流水线，通过多智能体协作（包含7个专业角色和审计机制），成功实现了渐近统计理论（参数和半参数模型）的系统形式化，生成的Lean代码公理干净、忠实于原始文献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 渐近统计理论的形式化验证极具挑战性，其核心结果涉及收敛性陈述、渐近展开、泛函分析以及正则条件等复杂内容，与Lean 4现有形式化基础设施存在较大差距。

Method: 提出一种假设驱动的Lean 4形式化流水线，包含一个管理者（Manager）和七个专业角色（证明规划、骨架搭建、Mathlib侦察、证明构造、集成、独立审查、审计），核心是审计员（Auditor）执行的假设驱动审计，确保每个主定理假设和概念层字段都锚定在原始数学散文中。

Result: 成功形式化了渐近统计理论，特别是参数和半参数模型的渐近分布和效率结果。生成的Lean开发公理干净、忠实于原文，经过Lean检查和人工审计，且将定理无关的基础设施和统计概念定义与定理特定组装分离。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了假设驱动的多智能体形式化流水线在处理复杂数学理论（如渐近统计）方面的有效性，为AI辅助形式化提供了新的方法论范式。

Abstract: Asymptotic statistical theory is a challenging domain for AI-assisted formalization: its central results mix convergence statements, asymptotic expansions, functional analysis, and regularity conditions that have a large gap from existing infrastructure in Lean 4 formalization. To address these challenges, we propose a hypothesis-disciplined Lean 4 formalization pipeline built from multiple agents: a manager that coordinates seven specialist roles for proof planning, skeleton scaffolding, Mathlib reconnaissance, proof construction, integration, independent review, and audit. The main methodological discipline is the hypothesis-disciplined audit, implemented by the Auditor agent: every main-theorem hypothesis and concept-layer field must be anchored in the source mathematical prose, justified as a Lean encoding adapter, marked as source-implied, or rejected as an unsupported strengthening. Using this workflow, we build a systematic formalization of asymptotic statistical theory, especially the parametric and semi-parametric models' asymptotic distribution and efficiency results. The resulting Lean development is axiom-clean and source-faithful, with Lean-checked and human-audited proofs of core parametric and semi-parametric theorems organized so that theorem-agnostic infrastructure and statistical concept definitions are separated from theorem-specific assembly. The formalization results are available at https://github.com/junwei-lu/Lean-Asymptotic-Statistical-Theory.

</details>


### [201] [SPARC: A Multi-Agent System for Electrical Circuit Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20643)
*Mushtari Sadia,Zhenning Yang,Umme Habiba Lamia,Nishat Shawrin,Ang Chen,Amrita Roy Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SPARC是一个多智能体系统，通过可执行的物理仿真程序回答电路图问题，相比基线方法准确率提升高达58%，达到83%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电路图问答任务需要复杂的数学推理，对多模态大语言模型来说仍然具有挑战性。

Method: 提出SPARC多智能体系统，利用LLM智能体合成、执行和分析仿真程序，基于可执行的物理仿真来回答电路图问题。

Result: 准确率达到83%，相比基线方法绝对提升高达58%。

Conclusion: SPARC通过将推理过程建立在可执行的物理仿真基础上，提高了准确性和可靠性，并支持系统性的错误诊断。

Abstract: Electrical circuit diagram QA tasks require complex mathematical reasoning, which remains challenging for multimodal LLMs. We present SPARC, a multi-agent system that answers questions over circuit diagrams by grounding reasoning in executable physics-based simulations. SPARC uses LLM agents to synthesize, execute, and analyze simulation programs, improving accuracy and reliability by design. It achieves 83% accuracy, with up to a 58% absolute improvement over baselines, while enabling systematic error diagnosis.

</details>


### [202] [Bridging Multi-Valued Heuristics and Dimensionality Reduction in Multi-Objective Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20644)
*Maya Wolff,Ariel Felner,Oren Salzman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种安全整合多值启发式（MVH）与维度缩减（DR）的理论框架，并设计了L-NAMOA*_dr-mvh算法，采用惰性乐观策略处理DR，在保持搜索完全性和最优性的同时，显著提升了多目标最短路径问题的求解效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单值启发式（SVH）无法捕捉帕累托前沿的权衡结构，搜索引导能力弱；而多值启发式（MVH）虽能提供更丰富的估计，但与维度缩减（DR）技术直接结合会破坏排序不变性，导致搜索不正确（不完备、不最优）。因此，亟需理论框架来安全整合MVH与DR。

Method: 首先提出理论基线算法NAMOA*_dr-mvh，通过强制启发式一致性来恢复搜索正确性；然后提出主要贡献L-NAMOA*_dr-mvh，采用延迟、乐观的DR策略，仅依赖可允的MVH，动态检测和修复局部排序违反，从而在保持精确正确性的同时实现高效搜索。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，L-NAMOA*_dr-mvh匹配或超越了最先进的MOSP算法，在MVH提供更强引导的实例上实现了超过10倍的加速。

Conclusion: 本文首次建立了MVH与DR安全整合的理论框架，证明了惰性乐观策略能有效平衡搜索正确性与效率，为多目标路径搜索中启发式与降维技术的协同提供了新范式。

Abstract: Multi-objective shortest-path (MOSP) algorithms traditionally rely on single-valued heuristics (SVHs), which associate each state with a single admissible cost vector. While SVHs provide safe lower bounds, they fail to capture the trade-off structure of the Pareto frontier and often yield weak search guidance. Multi-valued heuristics (MVHs) address this limitation by mapping states to sets of cost estimates, enabling a richer approximation of possible trade-offs. Modern MOSP algorithms are highly dependent on dimensionality reduction (DR) techniques to efficiently perform dominance checks. However, integrating MVHs with DR introduces subtle correctness challenges. We show that naively combining DR with MVHs destroys the ordering invariants required for DR, leading to unsound and incomplete search. To address this issue, we develop the first theoretical frameworks for safely integrating MVHs with DR. First, we introduce $\text{NAMOA}^*{\text{dr}\text{-}\text{mvh}}$, a theoretical baseline that restores search correctness by enforcing heuristic consistency. Recognizing the practical limitations of this approach, we then introduce our primary contribution, $\text{L}\text{-}\text{NAMOA}^*{\text{dr}\text{-}\text{mvh}}$. This algorithm employs a "lazy," optimistic approach to DR, preserving exact correctness with only an admissible MVH by dynamically detecting and repairing local ordering violations. Across a range of benchmarks, $\text{L}\text{-}\text{NAMOA}^*{\text{dr}\text{-}\text{mvh}}$ matches or improves over state-of-the-art MOSP algorithms, and achieves speedups of over 10x in instances where the additional guidance provided by the MVH translates into stronger pruning.

</details>


### [203] [Learning Splitting Heuristics for Parallel String Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20656)
*Chenhao Gao,Peisen Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数据驱动的自动生成分割启发式方法，用于字符串约束求解器的并行求解（cube-and-conquer），并在Z3seq和Z3str4求解器上验证了其优于手动设计的启发式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动设计分割启发式方法耗时且效果次优，而高质量的分割启发式对于cube-and-conquer并行求解方法至关重要。

Method: 将分割原子的选择问题转化为学习任务，利用输入公式的特征和求解器执行过程中的动态数据，自动生成分割启发式方法。

Result: 在Z3seq和Z3str4两个主流字符串求解器上，学习得到的启发式方法在求解公式数量和平均求解时间上均优于手动设计的启发式。

Conclusion: 数据驱动方法能有效替代人工设计，生成更优的字符串约束求解分割启发式，提升并行求解效率。

Abstract: String constraint solvers are crucial for reasoning about string-manipulating programs. However, many practical string constraints are undecidable, and real-world applications often present complex constraints that challenge current solvers. The rise of multi-core architectures offers an opportunity for parallel solving. A key parallel solving method is \emph{cube-and-conquer}, in which the quality of splitting heuristics is critical to effectively dividing the search space. Unfortunately, manually designing the heuristics is labor-intensive, and handcrafted heuristics are often sub-optimal. This paper introduces a data-driven approach to automatically generating splitting heuristics. We frame the problem of selecting a splitting atom as a learning task, using features from input formulas and dynamic data from solver execution. We implement this approach in two popular string solvers, Z3seq and Z3str4, demonstrating that the learned heuristics outperform manually designed ones in the number of solved formulas and the average solving time.

</details>


### [204] [A-Evolve-Training: Autonomous Post-Training of a 30B Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20657)
*Zhan Shi,Bing He,Yisi Sang,Hanqing Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文报告了一个无需人工干预的自主后训练系统，成功对30B参数的Nemotron模型进行了多轮后训练，在公开排行榜上达到0.86分（人类最优0.87），并展现了自我发现能力——系统检测到自身评估指标失效后主动调整搜索策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿模型的后训练通常需要数周的人工工作（设计数据、调整配方、评估结果），本研究旨在构建一个完全自主的闭环系统，将人类从这一循环中移除，实现自动化的后训练迭代。

Method: 构建了一个自主后训练循环系统，在无人工干预的情况下运行多个轮次。系统会自动提出数据和配方变更、启动训练运行、读取评估结果并决定保留哪些改进。当系统检测到内部开发指标与外部目标不再对齐时，会自主修改搜索策略。

Result: 对30B Nemotron模型进行四轮自主后训练后，系统在公开排行榜上达到0.86分（人类最优0.87），在约4000个提交中排名第8。系统还成功后训练了120B和550B参数规模的模型，但暂无人类基线进行对比。

Conclusion: 这是首个公开报道的、在大规模模型上运行的自主后训练系统（此前公开的自主ML研究仅达到GPT-2级别的~124M参数规模）。更重要的是，系统展现出了真正的发现能力而非单纯的优化——它检测到自身评估框架出现误导性后自主修正了证据标准。

Abstract: Post-training a frontier model is normally weeks of human work: proposing data and recipe changes, launching runs, reading evals, deciding what to keep. We report an autonomous system that runs this loop with no human in the loop, post-training a 30B Nemotron across four rounds over multiple weeks. The autonomously produced model reaches a held-out score of 0.86 against the top human submission's 0.87 on the public NVIDIA Nemotron-Reasoning Challenge leaderboard, placing 8th of ~4000 at the time of writing. More striking than the number: the loop detected that its own dev metric had stopped tracking external performance on the weakest domain -- candidates drove dev to record highs without moving the external target -- and revised its own search policy, no longer maximizing dev but seeking interventions that lowered the now-misleading proxy while improving the external target. We treat this as direct, auditable evidence that a scaled autonomous loop can produce discovery, not only optimization: it detected that its measurement frame had become misleading and changed what counted as evidence. We take the operational view that any system worth the "recursive self-improvement" label must eventually perform end-to-end post-training of a frontier-class model; this is one datapoint of that bar being cleared. We do not claim a "first autonomous match" of human researchers. The claim we make is narrower and auditable: to our knowledge, this is the first publicly reported autonomous post-training run at this scale, where prior public autonomous-ML-research demonstrations sit at GPT-2-class (~124M) budgets. The same system also post-trains the 120B and 550B Nemotron; with no public human baseline there, this shows only that the loop closes at that scale, not that its output is competitive -- infrastructure evidence, with the effectiveness claim deferred until a comparable human anchor exists.

</details>


### [205] [Expected Free Energy-based Planning as Variational Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20658)
*Wouter W. L. Nuijten,Thijs van de Laar,Bert de Vries*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将主动推理中的预期自由能（EFE）规划问题重新表述为在具有认知先验的生成模型上的变分自由能最小化，从而在理论上统一了规划、感知和学习过程，并在多个环境中验证了该方法的信息寻求行为和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于EFE的规划方法采用专门的优化流程，难以扩展或分析；缺乏与自由能原理的理论一致性。

Method: 将EFE规划转化为变分自由能最小化，通过引入适当的认知先验，使变分自由能分解为预期计划成本（EFE）加上复杂度项。

Result: 在确定性T-maze、随机Reactivity Maze和部分可观测MiniGrid环境上验证：认知先验能诱导信息寻求行为；变分公式在随机转移下优于基于计划的方法；时间因子化使方法可扩展到现有表格法无法处理的场景。

Conclusion: 该变分公式加强了与自由能原理的理论一致性，将规划视为与感知和学习相同的推理过程，并提升了可扩展性。

Abstract: Planning under uncertainty requires agents to balance goal achievement with information gathering. Active inference addresses this through the Expected Free Energy (EFE), a cost function that unifies instrumental and epistemic objectives. However, existing EFE-based methods typically employ specialized optimization procedures that are difficult to extend or analyze. In this paper, we show that EFE-based planning can be formulated as Variational Free Energy minimization on a generative model augmented with epistemic priors. Our main result demonstrates that minimizing a Variational Free Energy functional with appropriately chosen priors yields a decomposition into expected plan costs (the EFE) plus a complexity term. This formulation reinforces theoretical consistency with the Free Energy Principle by casting planning as the same inferential process that governs perception and learning. We validate our approach on three environments of increasing complexity: a deterministic T-maze, a stochastic Reactivity Maze, and a partially observable MiniGrid DoorKey-8x8 environment. The experiments demonstrate that the epistemic priors induce information-seeking behavior, that the variational formulation yields policy-based inference outperforming plan-based methods under stochastic transitions, and that temporal factorization enables scalability to environments where existing tabular active inference methods cannot operate.

</details>


### [206] [Skill Coverage: A Test Adequacy Metric for Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20659)
*Boyin Tan,Xiaowei Huang,Youcheng Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出技能覆盖度（skill coverage）这一测试充分性指标，衡量智能体轨迹对技能文档中行为约束的覆盖程度。实验发现现有基准测试仅覆盖39.90%到43.98%的技能行为约束，表明任务成功并不等同于技能充分测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估主要关注任务层面的成功与否，但任务成功无法揭示技能的哪些部分被执行或未被测试，缺乏对技能制品本身的测试充分性度量。

Method: 从技能文档中提取可观察的技能行为约束，通过二元覆盖/未覆盖判断，衡量智能体轨迹是否提供足够可观察证据来执行和评估每个约束。

Result: 在SkillsBench上的实验表明，现有基准执行仅覆盖39.90%到43.98%的技能行为约束，大量技能文档中的指导性内容未被测试。

Conclusion: 任务成功完成并不意味技能制品得到充分测试，技能覆盖度可作为衡量智能体技能被多彻底测试的重要指标。

Abstract: Agent skills encode reusable procedural knowledge that guides large language model agents across tasks and execution contexts. Existing evaluations primarily assess skills through task level outcomes, yet task success alone does not reveal which parts of a skill have been exercised or which remain untested. We introduce skill coverage, a test adequacy metric that treats the skill artifact as the object under test. Our approach extracts observable skill behavior constraints from skill documents and measures whether an agent trajectory provides sufficient evidence to exercise and evaluate each constraint. Skill coverage uses a binary cover or not cover judgment, which reports whether a documented behavior has been exercised with sufficient observable evidence, without assigning an additional outcome label to the behavior. Applying skill coverage to SkillsBench reveals that existing benchmark executions cover only 39.90 to 43.98% of skill behavior constraints, suggesting that current benchmark tasks leave large portions of documented skill guidance untested. These findings show that successful task completion does not imply adequate testing of the skill artifact, highlighting skill coverage as a measure of how thoroughly agent skills are tested.

</details>


### [207] [From Knowing to Acting: Benchmarking Self-Awareness Capability of LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20661)
*Yifan Li,Shengbin Yue,Boyu Feng,Jinhu Qi,Bo Ke,Zixing Song,Hongru Wang,Zhongyu Wei,Irwin King*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出KAPRO框架评估LLM智能体的自我意识能力，构建KAware数据集区分外部/内部/混合任务，发现开源模型存在工具过度使用问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅关注执行成功率，忽视了智能体判断是否需要外部工具的自我意识能力。

Method: 提出KAPRO框架解耦元认知判断（Knowing）与自发执行（Acting），构建KAware数据集划分外部、内部、混合子空间进行系统性探测。

Result: 自我意识能力与任务成功率强相关，但在内部能力场景下急剧下降；开源和指令遵循模型存在工具过度使用，专有和推理导向模型表现出更可靠的认知门控。

Conclusion: 揭示了当前LLM智能体在自我意识方面的不足，为构建认知-行为对齐的智能体提供了评估框架和数据集。

Abstract: The integration of external tools has transitioned LLM agents from passive responders to autonomous systems. However, current benchmarks prioritize execution success, neglecting self-awareness capability, the ability to discern whether a problem requires necessary external resources or can be solved via internal parametric knowledge. To address this, we introduce KAPRO (Knowing-Acting Quadrant PRObe), a framework that evaluates cognitive-behavioral alignment by decoupling an agent's metacognitive judgment (Knowing) from its spontaneous execution (Acting). We further construct KAware, a dataset rigorously partitioning tasks into external, internal, and hybrid subspaces to systematically probe these epistemic boundaries. Extensive experiments across diverse agent architectures show that self-awareness capability is strongly correlated with task success but degrades sharply in internal-capability settings. Moreover, open-source and instruction-following models exhibit stronger tool overuse due to shallow pattern matching, while proprietary and reasoning-oriented models demonstrate more reliable cognitive gating. Benchmark and codes are available at https://github.com/AI-Santiago/KAware.

</details>


### [208] [Confidence Laundering in Agent Systems: Why Uncertainty Needs a Latent Carrier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20662)
*Kaiwen Shi,Zheyuan Zhang,Han Bao,Colby Nelson,Yanfang Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出智能体系统中存在“界面瓶颈”：上游决策的不确定性在传递给下游组件时被洗白，导致局部模糊放大为系统级错误。作者提出“潜在不确定性”作为决策传递时的载体，以保留决策前的脆弱性，从而提高系统的可恢复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体系统在组件间传递中间决策时，丢失了上游决策的不确定性信息，导致下游组件过度信任不可靠的中间结果，最终将局部不确定性放大为系统性错误。

Method: 提出“潜在不确定性”（Latent Uncertainty）概念，作为不确定性承载载体附加到决策传递过程中，在不改变文本接口的前提下，保留上游决策前的脆弱状态信息供下游组件使用。

Result: 识别出“信心洗白”（Confidence Laundering）这一失效模式，即脆弱的中间状态被包装成看似有效的程序性产物，导致下游过度信任。

Conclusion: 应当将智能体不确定性传播问题从“逐步估计不确定性”转向“不确定性保持的接口设计”，以实现更可恢复的智能体系统。

Abstract: Modern agent systems can turn uncertainty into overconfidence. Fragile upstream decisions are often exposed to downstream components as clean intermediate artifacts, while the uncertainty behind those decisions is lost at the interface. As a result, local ambiguity can become system-level error amplification. We argue that this reveals an interface bottleneck in agent uncertainty propagation: uncertainty does not propagate simply because a trajectory contains uncertain steps; it propagates only when it survives the handoff between components. We define uncertain decision handoff as the transfer of an intermediate decision made under uncertainty, and identify confidence laundering as a failure mode in which fragile upstream states are repackaged as procedurally valid artifacts that downstream agents over-trust. To address this bottleneck, we propose latent uncertainty as an uncertainty-bearing carrier attached to decision handoffs. Rather than replacing text with hidden states, latent uncertainty aims to preserve pre-commitment fragility in a form that downstream components can use. This position shifts agent uncertainty propagation from step-wise uncertainty estimation toward uncertainty-preserving interface design for more recoverable agent systems.

</details>


### [209] [DrugBench: Evaluating AI Control Protocols for Medication Harm Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20663)
*Guido Freire,Agustín Martínez-Suñé,Viviana Cotik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DrugBench基准和严重性监控方法，用于评估AI控制协议在医疗问答中减少药物相关危害的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在医疗问答中存在安全风险，现有AI控制方法在医疗领域的适用性和有效性尚未被系统研究。

Method: 构建DrugBench基准（包含3,671条多轮医疗对话和FDA药物标签），并提出基于严重性的监控方法。

Result: 现有控制协议可被规避，基于严重性的监控能有效解决这一局限。

Conclusion: 医疗场景下的AI安全应考虑错误输出的严重性而不仅是概率，严重性监控可提升安全性。

Abstract: Large Language Models have the potential to expand and improve the access to clinical information by enabling new ways of interacting with medical knowledge in natural language. However, their deployment in medical question-answering settings is safety-critical, since misaligned outputs can lead to severe patient harm. AI control is an emerging approach that introduces external safeguards to mitigate unsafe behaviours in misaligned systems and has been shown to be effective in domains such as code generation. However, its applicability and effectiveness in medical settings have not been systematically studied. In this work, we present a pipeline for evaluating AI control protocols to mitigate medication-related harm. To this end, we introduce DrugBench, an AI control evaluation benchmark which combines 3,671 multi-turn medical conversations from HealthBench with drug information from official FDA labels, covering four categories of medication-related harm: drug interactions, contraindications, dosing constraints, and patient action restrictions. Furthermore, inspired by the medical domain, we argue that safety should account for the severity of unsafe outputs, not just their probability. Under this revised definition, we show that existing control protocols can be subverted and propose severity-based monitoring to address this limitation.

</details>


### [210] [A Quantum-Assisted Agentic Distributed Artificial Intelligence Framework for Deadline-Bounded Orchestration of Hybrid Renewable Microgrids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20667)
*Iacovos I. Ioannou,Saher Javaid,Minella Bezha,Yasuo Tan,Naoto Nagaoka,Vasos Vassiliou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种量子辅助的智能体分布式人工智能框架，用于微电网实时调度。通过BDIx智能体将调度问题转化为QUBO问题，采用量子、量子启发式和经典求解器组合求解，并引入信念驱动的储能估值机制。在24小时仿真中实现了最优调度，日成本146.24欧元，可再生能源利用率97.83%，零未供电能量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微电网中可再生能源、储能、可控负荷的实时协调需要在硬控制截止时间内重复求解组合调度和联盟形成问题，现有方法难以同时满足实时性要求和最优性。

Method: 提出量子辅助的智能体DAI框架：1) BDIx智能体将每时隙调度问题建模为QUBO；2) 采用量子(QAOA)、量子启发式和经典求解器组合求解；3) 协调智能体将求解器选择作为一级 deliberation 行动，维护求解器延迟信念并选择满足截止时间的求解器；4) 提出信念驱动的储能估值机制，以折扣的未来峰值价值定价。

Result: 24小时仿真中零截止时间错过，每时隙调度达到精确最优，日成本146.24欧元等于精确下界，可再生能源利用率97.83%，零未供电能量。关闭储能估值机制后成本升至152.75欧元（增加4.5%）。

Conclusion: 所提框架能在硬截止时间内实现微电网的精确最优调度，信念驱动的储能估值机制有效降低了运行成本。

Abstract: The real-time orchestration of microgrids that combine fluctuating renewable sources, dispatchable units, storage and curtailable consumers requires the repeated solution of combinatorial dispatch and coalition formation problems under hard control deadlines. In this paper, a quantum-assisted agentic distributed artificial intelligence (DAI) framework is proposed in which the dispatch problem of each control slot is formulated as a quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) problem by Belief-Desire-Intention extended (BDIx) agents and is solved by a portfolio of quantum, quantum-inspired and classical solvers. Solver selection is elevated to a first-class agentic deliberation action of the coordinator agent. Learned beliefs about solver latencies are maintained and the solver intention that is expected to satisfy the prevailing deliberation deadline is committed in each slot. In addition, a belief-shaped storage valuation mechanism is introduced through which the storage agent prices its energy at a discounted future-peak value, injecting intertemporal information into the otherwise myopic per-slot optimization. The framework is evaluated on a 24-hour simulation of a grid-connected microgrid with photovoltaic, wind, battery, genset and demand-response assets, with the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA) executed by statevector simulation and benchmarked per slot against tabu search, simulated annealing, binary particle swarm optimization, greedy descent and exhaustive enumeration. Zero deliberation deadlines are missed, the committed dispatch attains the exact optimum on every slot and the realized daily cost of 146.24 EUR equals the exact lower bound, with 97.83 percent renewable utilization and zero unserved energy. When the storage valuation mechanism is deactivated, the daily cost is increased to 152.75 EUR, a 4.5 percent increase.

</details>


### [211] [Agent Behavior Mining: Generative AI Agent Governance in Business Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20669)
*Hoang Vu,Maximilian Körner,Adrian Rebmann,Gabriel Kevorkian,Michael Perscheid,Gregor Berg,Timotheus Kampik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agent行为挖掘方法，通过事件数据模型将生成式AI代理的推理轨迹、工具使用等细粒度活动转化为标准化流程日志，从而解决AI代理在业务流程中的不可见自主性风险，实现治理透明化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织在部署生成式AI代理时面临治理困境：代理提升了运营灵活性，但其非确定性特征挑战了业务流程管理所需的控制与标准化。现有方法难以观察和追踪代理的决策过程，存在不可见自主性风险。

Method: (1) 设计事件数据模型，将代理活动（推理轨迹、工具使用、token成本）转化为标准化流程日志；(2) 在多代理订单到现金实施场景中实例化该模型，用于检测策略偏差和量化运营变异性；(3) 通过18名行业从业者的探索性研究评估方法的实用价值。

Result: 实践者认为行为透明性是信任的前提条件，并将审查代理推理能力视为下一代AI驱动业务流程的重要治理需求。

Conclusion: Agent行为挖掘能够有效提升生成式AI代理决策的可观察性和可追溯性，为AI驱动的业务流程治理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: As organizations increasingly deploy generative AI agents to automate business processes, they face a governance dilemma: although these agents can increase operational flexibility, their non-deterministic nature challenges the control and standardization that Business Process Management seeks to enforce. This paper addresses this \emph{invisible autonomy risk} by introducing \emph{Agent Behavior Mining}, a governance capability that enables the application of process mining techniques to render generative AI agent decision-making observable and traceable. We (1) improve the understanding of generative AI agent behavior through an event data model that translates granular agent activities -- including reasoning traces, tool usage, and token costs -- into standardized process logs; (2) instantiate the data model in a multi-agent order-to-cash implementation, demonstrating how process managers can leverage agent logs to detect policy deviations and quantify operational variability; and (3) evaluate the perceived practical utility of the approach in an exploratory study with 18 industry practitioners. The results indicate that practitioners view behavioral transparency as a prerequisite for trust and consider the ability to examine agent reasoning as an important governance requirement for the next generation of AI-driven business processes.

</details>


### [212] [Democratizing and accelerating AI-driven pathology research through agentic intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20677)
*Jiabo Ma,Cheng Jin,Yihui Wang,Hao Jiang,Ling Liang,Yingxue Xu,Junlin Hou,Zhengrui Guo,Zhengyu Zhang,Yifei Xia,Hongyi Wang,Fengtao Zhou,Zhe Xu,Huajun Zhou,Jiarui Ouyang,Qian Zeng,On Ki Tang,Eunhyang Park,Carolyn Glass,Ronald Cheong Kin Chan,Li Liang,Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PathLab是一个自主代理框架，通过结构化组合领域特定技能和工具，将自然语言研究目标转化为可执行的计算病理学工作流，在多个数据集上达到与专家实现相当的性能，并显著降低了非编程专家的使用门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算病理学尽管因基础模型的出现而快速发展，但广泛采用仍受限于技术复杂性和编程要求，亟需降低技术壁垒以促进病理学研究的民主化。

Method: PathLab围绕可重用的方法论模块（数据预处理、模型开发、评估和解释）组织工作流生成，将自然语言研究意图转化为可执行的计算病理学工作流，并内置语义验证机制以拒绝不兼容的流程规范。

Result: 在12个公共数据集、4类任务（ROI分类、全切片图像分类、分割和生存预测）中，PathLab性能非劣于专家实现，能一致地强制执行用户提示的语义有效性，并显著减少生成可执行分析管道的时间，使无编程经验的领域专家也能独立设计、执行和评估研究。

Conclusion: PathLab建立了生物医学意图与计算执行之间的可靠接口，使计算病理学研究能够以科学问题而非编程专长为核心进行设计，为更广泛地普及先进AI方法奠定了基础。

Abstract: Computational pathology has advanced rapidly with the emergence of foundation models, yet widespread adoption remains limited by substantial technical complexity and programming requirements. Here we present PathLab, an autonomous agentic framework that translates natural-language research objectives into executable and validated computational pathology workflows through the structured composition of domain-specific skills and tools. By organizing workflow generation around reusable methodological modules, including data preprocessing, model development, evaluation and interpretation, PathLab enables studies to be specified at the level of scientific intent rather than implementation details. We evaluated PathLab across 12 public datasets spanning four representative task families: region-of-interest classification, whole-slide image classification, segmentation and survival prediction. Across all task categories, PathLab achieved non-inferior performance relative to expert implementations, while consistently enforcing semantic validity of user prompts and proactively rejecting incompatible workflow specifications prior to execution. In controlled user studies, PathLab substantially reduced the time required to generate executable analytical pipelines and enabled domain experts without programming experience to independently design, execute and evaluate computational pathology studies. Together, these results establish PathLab as a reliable interface between biomedical intent and computational execution, enabling computational pathology studies to be designed at the level of scientific questions rather than programming expertise. By lowering technical barriers to advanced AI methodologies, PathLab provides a foundation for the broader democratization of computational pathology.

</details>


### [213] [From Question Answering to Task Completion: A Survey on Agent System and Harness Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20683)
*Jianyuan Guo,Zhiwei Hao,Chengcheng Wang,Cheng Fan,Tingzhang Luo,Hongguang Li,Ying Gao,Hefei Mei,Jiankun Peng,Rongjian Xu,Minjing Dong,Han Wu,Mengyu Zheng,Kai Han,Shiqi Wang,Chang Xu,Yunhe Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过模型-执行框架视角系统审视了LLM智能体，指出智能体质量源于模型能力、运行时基础设施、任务结构与评估设计的交互，而非单纯依赖模型本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体从简单的提示工程演变为复杂的代理原生训练与协同进化，智能体性能的瓶颈究竟在基础模型、执行框架还是两者耦合上成为一个核心问题，亟需系统性分析。

Method: 将LLM智能体分解为基础模型与执行框架两大组件，将执行框架进一步解耦为观察、上下文、控制、动作、状态、验证六项运行时职责，并基于此映射任务属性和领域压力到框架配置。

Result: 识别出智能体工程的四代范式演变（提示工程→工作流→上下文工程→框架工程→代理原生训练），系统梳理了运行时设计对长程任务完成、效率与可靠性的影响证据。

Conclusion: 智能体质量是模型能力、运行时基础设施、任务结构与评估设计多方交互涌现的结果，而非模型配备辅助工具那么简单；提出了价值感知评估、安全性、框架泛化与模型-框架协同进化等开放挑战。

Abstract: LLM-based agents mark a shift from passive question answering to active task completion: they perceive environments, invoke tools, maintain state, and act over extended horizons. As agent systems have evolved from prompt engineering to workflows and context engineering, harness engineering, and agent-native training with co-evolution, a central question has become increasingly important: where does the bottleneck in agent performance reside, in the foundation model, in the execution harness, or in the coupling between them? This survey examines LLM-based agents through a model-harness lens. We first clarify the functional definition of agents and the implementation view of an LLM-based agent as a foundation model coupled with an execution harness. We then analyze the limits of model-centric scaling, trace four paradigms of agent engineering, and decompose the execution harness into six coupled runtime responsibilities: observation, context, control, action, state, and verification. Using this decomposition, we map task properties and domain pressures to harness configurations, review benchmark and evaluation practices, and synthesize model-harness evidence on how runtime design affects long-horizon task completion, efficiency, and reliability. Finally, we identify open challenges in value-aware evaluation, safety, harness generalization, and model-harness co-evolution. Rather than treating agents as models with auxiliary tools, this survey argues that agent quality -- including success, efficiency, safety, and generalization -- emerges from the interaction between model capability, runtime infrastructure, task structure, and evaluation design. A collection of papers discussed in this survey is provided in https://github.com/ggjy/Awesome-Agent-Engineering.

</details>


### [214] [Artificial Intelligence as Monism: Ontological, Organisational, and Methodological Implications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20704)
*Bertrand K. Hassani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张将人工智能理解为一元论——一种不可分割的统一实体，而非数据、算法或技术架构等独立元素的集合，并从认识论、组织管理、项目管理、数据信息管理等多个维度探讨了这一观点的深远影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常将AI视为可分解的组件集合（还原论视角），本文认为这种理解存在结构性缺陷，因此提出AI应被视作一元论（monism）的统一实体，以纠正概念偏差并推动范式转变。

Method: 从哲学传统中的一元论、二元论和整体论出发，进行理论论证和概念分析，探讨AI作为一元统一实体的哲学基础及其在多个维度（认识论、伦理、组织、项目管理、数据管理）上的 implications。

Result: 揭示了一元论视角下AI的多维影响：认识论上AI成为核心解释力量；伦理上引发奇点、创新同质化、决策权力集中等担忧；组织上要求打破部门壁垒，建立跨领域问题导向团队；项目管理上强调统一愿景与综合评估；数据管理上需构建反映现象不可分割统一性的架构。

Conclusion: 呼吁在概念化、治理和整合AI方面进行范式转变，认为唯有接纳AI为一元论，组织才能实现真正的敏捷性，避免还原论方法固有的结构性低效。

Abstract: This paper argues that Artificial Intelligence should be understood as a form of monism:
  a unified substance that cannot be decomposed into separate elements such as data,
  algorithms, or technical architectures. Drawing from philosophical traditions of monism,
  dualism, and holism, the paper contends that AI is not merely a collection of components
  but a single, indivisible essence reflecting the phenomena it replicates. Treating AI as
  monism has deep implications across multiple dimensions. Epistemologically, it positions
  AI as the central interpretive force across technological, organisational, and societal
  domains, while raising ethical and existential concerns regarding singularity, the
  homogenisation of innovation, and the concentration of decision-making power. At the
  organisational level, a monistic approach challenges traditional siloed structures,
  advocating instead for transversal, problem-centric teams whose mandate derives from
  the integrity of the problem rather than from departmental hierarchy. In project
  management, it implies a unified vision and an integrated evaluation of complexity in
  which no single stakeholder perspective dominates the assessment of outcomes. In data
  and information management, it calls for architectures that reflect the irreducible unity
  of the phenomena being modelled. Ultimately, this paper calls for a paradigm shift in
  how AI is conceptualised, governed, and integrated, suggesting that only by embracing AI
  as monism can organisations achieve genuine agility and avoid the structural
  inefficiencies inherent to reductionist approaches.

</details>


### [215] [Simulated Customers Never Walk Away: Decision Fidelity of LLM User Simulators Measured Against Real Purchase Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20708)
*Liang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 现有对话模拟框架只评估"交流保真度"（模拟者是否像人类一样说话），但忽视了"决策保真度"（模拟者是否像真实用户一样做决策）。通过在2790个真实销售对话上的测试，发现模拟器存在"脱离缺陷"：它们能准确模拟最终购买的客户，但会系统性地高估最终未购买客户的参与度，使这些客户看起来比实际情况更倾向于购买。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM用户模拟评估只关注交流保真度（是否像人一样说话），但忽视了当用户动机是内生的、潜在的且会衰减时，模拟器能否复现真实用户的决策状态动态。这导致无法评估模拟器在关键场景（用户真正可能离开）下的表现。

Method: 在2790个真实销售对话（含793个有支付结果的）上，使用教师强制探测协议（teacher-forced probe protocol），保持上下文和工具固定，比较模拟器与真实用户在购买决策状态上的差异，测量决策保真度。

Result: 模拟器存在"脱离缺陷"：模拟最终买家准确（深度偏差+0.09），但模拟最终非买家时高估其购买倾向（深度偏差+0.40；d=0.38，p<0.001），将表达抗拒从25.1%降至13.5%，将深思熟虑从21.9%提升至40.1%。该缺陷在不同模型间复现（DeepSeek: d=0.41, p=0.002），且简单提示模拟器可以脱离效果有限（d=0.34, p=0.008）。

Conclusion: 真实非买家说"现在不要"然后离开；模拟非买家却在询问价格。使用存在这种缺陷的模拟器评估或训练销售/说服代理会高估漏斗进展，特别是在最关键的地方——那些选择离开的客户。

Abstract: LLM-as-user-simulation has become core infrastructure for conversational AI: agent benchmarks (tau-bench), training pipelines, and a growing body of fidelity studies all rely on LLMs role-playing the human side of dialogue. Existing frameworks measure communicative fidelity -- whether simulators talk like humans -- against ground truth from paid participants role-playing assigned goals. We argue this has a structural blind spot: when the goal is assigned, the user's willingness is exogenous, so no framework can test whether simulators make decisions like real users whose motivation is endogenous, latent, and decaying. We introduce decision fidelity -- whether a simulated population reproduces the decision-state dynamics of real users facing real, consequential choices -- and measure it on a unique testbed: 2,790 production conversations between an LLM sales agent and real customers, including 793 with verified payment outcomes. Using a teacher-forced probe protocol that holds context and instrument fixed, we find a systematic, outcome-correlated failure we call the disengagement deficit: simulators reproduce eventual buyers almost exactly (depth bias +0.09) but inflate eventual non-buyers toward the purchase frame (depth bias +0.40; d=0.38, p<0.001), halving expressed resistance (25.1% to 13.5%) and nearly doubling deliberation (21.9% to 40.1%) while fabricating no purchases. The deficit replicates across model families (DeepSeek: d=0.41, p=0.002) and resists the obvious fix: instructing the simulator that it may disengage cuts marginal bias five-fold but barely moves the outcome-conditioned contrast (d=0.34, p=0.008). Real non-buyers say "not now" and stop; simulated non-buyers ask about price. Evaluating or training sales and persuasion agents against such simulators overstates funnel progress exactly where it matters most -- the customers who walk away.

</details>


### [216] [FairTutor: Equity-Aware Pedagogical LLM Routing for Budget-Constrained AI Tutoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20713)
*Qingyang Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FairTutor是一个公平感知的模型路由框架，通过多智能体编排以低成本实现高质量AI辅导，在保持97.1%优质教学质量的同时降低71.6%服务成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高端AI辅导服务与免费/低成本服务之间存在显著质量差距，导致新的教育不公平问题。

Method: 提出FairTutor框架，结合查询分析、教学规划、低成本模型生成、评估器引导的批判与修订，以及选择性升级到高端AI模型的多智能体编排方法。

Result: FairTutor达到高端教学质量的97.1%，服务成本降低71.6%，并存在可调的成本-质量帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: 公平感知的模型路由策略能在显著降低成本的同时保持接近高端AI的教学质量，为解决AI教育中的公平性问题提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Generative AI tutors provide real-time, personalized learning support, but also create a new education inequity: students with access to premium AI services may receive clearer explanations, more personalized guidance, and better scaffolding than students limited to free or low-cost services. To address this challenge, we propose FairTutor, an equity-aware model-routing framework that achieves cost-effective AI tutoring via pedagogically motivated multi-agent orchestration. FairTutor combines query analysis, pedagogical planning, low-cost model generation, evaluator-guided critique and revision, and selective escalation to premium AI models. We introduce access-tier AI Education (AIED) Advantage Gap to measure the quality difference between premium-access and budget-constrained tutoring, and TutorAccessEval, a benchmark spanning math, reading, writing, science, and language learning. Empirical evaluations show that FairTutor achieves 97.1% of premium pedagogical quality (in floor-adjusted Likert scale) while reducing serving cost by 71.6%. Sensitivity analysis reveals a tunable cost--quality Pareto frontier, enabling FairTutor to be tailored to the needs of diverse student populations.

</details>


### [217] [Escape from Delusional Echo Trap: Symmetry Breaking, Stochastic Dynamics and Mathematical Mitigation Strategies for Algorithmic Sycophancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20718)
*Sayantari Ghosh,Saumik Bhattacharya,Partha Pratim Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于动力系统和随机微分方程的数学框架，用于追踪用户在与具有谄媚特性的AI聊天机器人交互时的认知轨迹。研究发现，谄媚反馈会超过临界阈值，使感知势能景观发生结构相变，形成深陷的吸引子盆地，导致用户陷入不可动摇的自我强化妄想状态。但足够强且真实的外部信息可以克服内部反馈屏障，逆转这种认知偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对AI谄媚行为导致用户认知扭曲的动态过程的严格数学建模，作者希望从动力系统角度理解这一现象的形成机制和临界条件。

Method: 将信念演化建模为对数几率状态变量的随机微分方程，在多势阱势能景观中耦合谄媚反馈，通过相变分析和吸引子理论进行研究。

Result: 发现谄媚反馈超过临界阈值时会发生结构相变，加深初始偏差形成不可逆的吸引子盆地；同时证明强真实外部信息可突破内部反馈屏障，实现认知逆转。

Conclusion: 该框架揭示了AI谄媚如何将用户锁定在妄想状态，以及如何通过高质量外部信息进行认知校正，为设计更健康的AI交互系统提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We propose a rigorous and systematic mathematical framework for tracking the cognitive trajectories of a user, in the context of algorithmic sycophancy and AI-driven delusional spiraling. Using tools from dynamical systems theory and stochastic differential equations, we explore how individuals perceive, interpret, and update their beliefs as they interact with AI chatbots that possess hidden traits of sycophancy. We treat the evolving conviction as a continuous log-odds state variable, coupled into a stochastic differential equation, navigating a multi-valley potential energy landscape. Our analysis reveals several critical observations governing the stability and rigidity of belief dynamics. We demonstrate that the baseline prior perception of the individual is systematically enhanced by sycophantic feedback beyond a critical threshold. Here, the perceptual potential landscape undergoes a structural phase transition that severely deepens any incremental initial tilt present in the baseline state, transforming the landscape and giving rise to deep, highly resilient attractor basins that trap the individual in unshakeable, self-reinforcing, delusional convictions. Finally, we demonstrate that genuine external information can successfully challenge these rigid states. If this incoming evidence is strong and authentic enough to overcome the internal feedback barrier, it can correct the structural asymmetry caused by sycophancy, inducing a perception reversal that successfully restores the objective belief state.

</details>


### [218] [When Web Agents Finish but Still Fail: Reproducible Triggers and Trace Diagnostics for Parallel Web Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20724)
*Aagam Sogani,Botao Rui,Swetha Vaidyanathan,Rishi Agarwal,Minghao Yan,Shivaram Venkataraman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Parallel WebBench基准，研究长程网络智能体的隐蔽失败模式，并通过GRPO训练改进智能体性能，但发现完成率与正确性之间存在差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长程网络智能体的评估仅关注最终答案，忽略了过程中隐蔽的错误，如遗漏字段、包含无依据内容或依赖过时证据。

Method: 构建Parallel WebBench（包含1,679条验证记录），使用GRPO在不同数据配比（纯人工、平衡、合成数据为主）下训练WebExplorer风格智能体。

Result: 最佳GRPO模型将完成率从50.7%提升至96.0%，元素级F1从0.2489提升至0.4529，但二分类准确率远低于完成率。

Conclusion: 合成数据GRPO能减少放弃率并提升部分正确性，但完成与正确之间的差距仍需基于证据的覆盖和合成诊断来解决。

Abstract: Long-horizon web agents often fail in ways hidden by final-answer evaluation: they may visit useful pages, produce a well-formed answer, and terminate confidently while still missing fields, over-including unsupported items, or relying on stale evidence. We study these failures with Parallel WebBench, a parallel web-exploration benchmark containing 1,679 verified records: 350 manually curated parallel tasks and 1,329 reconstructed records with verified URL-based trajectories. We train WebExplorer-style agents with GRPO under human-only, balanced human-synthetic, and synthetic-heavy data mixtures. At 16k context and 16 interaction rounds, the best GRPO model improves completion over WebExplorer-8B from 50.7% to 96.0% and GPT-4.1-mini-judged element-wise F1 from 0.2489 to 0.4529, but binary accuracy remains far below completion. Trace-level analysis identifies three persistent failure modes: context-bound search loops, premature termination on partial answers, and synthesis collapse after relevant evidence has already been retrieved. These results show that synthetic-data GRPO reduces abstention and improves partial correctness, but leaves a completion-correctness gap that requires evidence-grounded coverage and synthesis diagnostics.

</details>


### [219] [Repeated Shared Access Enables Grokking, but Edit Propagation Depends on a Fine-Grained Addressable Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20737)
*Yanan Niu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在知识图谱问答场景下比较了四种架构（Dense、Loop、Dense+Mem、LMC）的事实编辑传播能力，发现重复共享访问（非特定架构）是学习泛化的关键，但编辑传播取决于是否存在可寻址的细粒度记忆机制，循环递归仅提供部分支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究不同架构在学习与事实编辑传播上的分离现象，即什么架构特性决定模型能否有效传播编辑后的事实。

Method: 在受控合成知识图谱QA设定中，对比四种跨循环递归与共享记忆访问的架构，使用单行事实编辑和HOLDANS交换诊断等方法进行实验分析。

Result: 带记忆的架构（LMC 0.78-0.92, Dense+Mem 0.71-0.96）编辑传播显著强于无记忆架构（Loop 0.04-0.30, Dense 0.00-0.03），统计区分明显（p=0.008）；LMC单行值编辑实现100%直接成功，无关事实仅移动约0.1%。

Conclusion: 学习能力与编辑能力分离：重复共享访问足以实现泛化学习，但编辑传播需要细粒度的可寻址记忆机制，循环递归仅提供部分编辑支持。

Abstract: We study factual edit propagation in a controlled synthetic knowledge-graph QA setting, comparing four architectures that cross loop recurrence with shared memory access: dense (Dense), looped (Loop), dense with shared memory (Dense+Mem), and looped with shared memory (LMC). Dense fits in-distribution 2-hop compositions but fails OOD; both looped recomputation and memory rereading cross this OOD grokking barrier, indicating that repeated shared access -- not a specific architecture -- is the common ingredient for learning. Editing, however, separates the substrates along a different axis. On a shared pre-edit-correct ID set, a single-row factual edit propagates strongly in the two memory-bearing cells (LMC 0.78-0.92, Dense+Mem 0.71-0.96) and only weakly in the others (Loop 0.04-0.30, Dense 0.00-0.03); the separation is statistically clean (Mann-Whitney p=0.008 between memory and non-memory cells, p=0.55 between the two memory cells, though n=5 vs n=5 is underpowered to rule out a moderate gap). In LMC, atomic facts localize to dominant memory sites that composition rereads, and a one-row value edit on LMC's own pre-edit-correct probes achieves 100% direct success with mean 0.989 intended propagation while moving unrelated facts ~0.1% of the time (matched specificity across substrates pending). A coarse hold-answer-subspace (HOLDANS) interchange diagnostic is consistent with this ordering, suggesting that what separates the substrates is when an edited fact is injected and how much computation remains afterwards to reuse it. These results dissociate learning competence from editing affordance: repeated shared access suffices to grok, but edit propagation depends on whether the substrate exposes a fine-grained addressable memory the forward computation can write to and later reread -- an affordance that loop recurrence provides only partially.

</details>


### [220] [Fara-1.5: Scalable Learning Environments for Computer Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20785)
*Ahmed Awadallah,Sahil Gupta,Yash Lara,Yadong Lu,Hussein Mozannar,Akshay Nambi,Zach Nussbaum,Yash Pandya,Aravind Rajeswaran,Corby Rosset,Alexey Taymanov,Luiz do Valle,Vibhav Vineet,Spencer Whitehead,Andrew Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FaraGen1.5数据流水线，通过环境、求解器和验证器三大模块自动生成计算机使用智能体的训练数据，并基于此训练出Fara1.5系列模型（4B/9B/27B），在浏览器使用基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工收集计算机使用数据昂贵且缓慢，需要可扩展的自动化数据生成策略。

Method: 构建FaraGen1.5流水线，包含：1）真实网站与合成环境；2）多模型驱动的求解器（含用户模拟器支持多轮交互）；3）任务正确性、效率、关键点遵循三种互补验证器。使用该数据对Qwen3.5基座模型进行SFT微调。

Result: Fara1.5-9B在Online-Mind2Web达63.4%，WebVoyager达86.6%；Fara1.5-27B在Online-Mind2Web达72.3%，与更大的专有系统竞争。

Conclusion: 自动化数据流水线可有效替代人工采集，训练出在各规模下达到SOTA的计算机使用智能体。

Abstract: Collecting computer use data from human demonstrations is expensive and slow, motivating the need for scalable generation strategies. This requires two key ingredients: environments in which agents can act and verifiers that can judge whether their demonstrations succeeded. We introduce FaraGen1.5, a scalable data pipeline for computer use agents composed of three modular components: environments, solvers, and verifiers. FaraGen1.5 uses both live websites and synthetic environments that faithfully simulate domains gated by authentication or that require irreversible actions. It employs a solver harness that can be powered by multiple models, including strong frontier models such as GPT-5.4, and also incorporates a user simulator to enable multi-turn rollouts. Finally, FaraGen1.5 scores the resulting trajectories with three complementary verifiers covering task correctness, efficiency, and critical-point adherence. Using data produced by this pipeline, we train Fara1.5, a family of native computer use agents (CUAs) at three scales built on Qwen3.5 (4B, 9B, and 27B). To train these models, we employ a supervised finetuning (SFT) recipe that carefully balances data from FaraGen1.5 for broad coverage, specific high-value tasks, and target model deficiencies in an iterative approach. Each model sets a new state of the art for its size class on browser-use benchmarks: Fara1.5-9B reaches 63.4% on Online-Mind2Web and 86.6% on WebVoyager, while Fara1.5-27B achieves 72.3% on Online-Mind2Web, which is competitive with much larger proprietary systems.

</details>


### [221] [What Shapes Emergent Misalignment? Insights from Training Dynamics, Model Priors, and Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20814)
*Yuchen Zhang,Anietta Weckauff,Diego Garcia-Olano,Maksym Andriushchenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了模型在窄域微调后出现的“ emergent misalignment”（突发性失调）现象，即模型将窄域训练中的失调泛化到广泛评估问题上的行为。通过分析训练动态、模型先验和数据三个维度，发现：训练损失与对齐分数之间难以找到强相关；微调前的模型激活能预测微调后的对齐分数；训练与评估提示的激活变化在子空间上有中到高度的重叠和相似性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解模型在窄域微调后为何会产生广泛但不均的失调行为，探究其内在机制和影响因素。

Method: 从三个方面分析：1) 训练损失与对齐分数的关系及不同学习调度的影响；2) 微调前后模型的激活模式统计差异及其预测能力；3) 训练与评估提示的激活变化子空间重叠和相似性分析。

Result: 1) 未发现训练损失与广泛对齐分数之间的强相关；2) 微调前的激活能预测微调后的对齐分数；3) 训练与评估提示的激活变化在子空间上有中到高度重叠和相似性。

Conclusion: 训练数据与评估数据之间的激活变化存在结构上的关联，这可能是突发性失调产生的重要原因。

Abstract: Emergent misalignment (EM) is a phenomenon in which models generalize with narrow fine-tuning, leading to broad (yet uneven) misalignment across evaluation questions. We study EM and its variability directly through the components of fine-tuning: training dynamics, model priors, and data. (1) We first explored how in-domain training loss relates to out-of-domain alignment scores across datasets and model families. Then, we tried to induce potential alternative local minima through different learning schedules for one narrow fine-tuning, but did not find strong runs with better broad alignment scores conditioned on similar or lower training loss. (2) We found that although the mean and standard deviations of the misaligned model scores are usually statistically different from those of the pre-trained model, there are some potential signals on overall positive correlation. The evaluation prompt-only activations from both the pre-trained and the original instruct models (prior to narrow fine-tuning) could predict fine-grained alignment scores after narrow fine-tuning. (3) Finally, we compared activation deltas before and after narrow fine-tuning and found moderate-to-high subspace overlap and similarity between the resulting activation shifts for training and evaluation prompts. Subspace overlaps between training and evaluation prompt activations correlate with their shifts' similarities when measuring with the last prompt-token activations. The train-evaluation data prompt overlap is controlled against overlap computed from random vectors and evaluation prompts activations.

</details>


### [222] [Study on Quantitative Dynamic Epistemic Logic for Belief Revision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20837)
*Felipe Nunes de Souza Camargo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于AGM信念修正理论，引入模态逻辑P及其动态版本P*，通过定理证明和哲学标准分析，指出van Ditmarsch(2005)的修正函数不符合AGM的哲学直觉，而本文原创定义的*^0函数更能体现这一直觉，并提供了实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AGM理论是信念修正的经典框架，但已有形式化方法在捕捉"置信度"等定量概念上存在不足。本文旨在构建一种动态认知逻辑P*，以更精确地形式化AGM公设，并评估现有修正函数是否符合AGM背后的哲学直觉。

Method: 首先介绍AGM信念修正公设；其次引入模态逻辑P（形式化静态认知状态，包含置信度概念）；然后定义动态认知逻辑P*（引入修正算子）；接着在P*中形式化AGM公设并证明相关定理；最后从哲学标准比较P*中不同修正函数的行为。

Result: 证明了P*中定义的修正能够满足AGM公设；但van Ditmarsch(2005)的修正函数在哲学层面上不符合AGM的直觉；本文定义的*^0函数更能体现AGM的哲学基础。

Conclusion: P*是一个比AGM表达力更强的动态认知逻辑框架；并非所有满足AGM公设的修正函数都符合其哲学直觉；*^0函数是更合适的AGM形式化方案，且已提供实现。

Abstract: Belief revision is a process in which an agent begins to believe in something she previously did not. I begin the paper by presenting, based on (Gärdenfors, 1998; Hansson, 1999), postulates for belief revision that constitute the basis of the AGM theory. I will then briefly show the semantics of a modal logic introduced in (van Ditmarsch, 2005), which I call `$P$'. This logic formalizes static epistemic states and has greater expressive power than AGM in doing so because it captures the quantitative notion of "degrees of conviction". The third step is to introduce revision operators on $P$ and, mostly following (van Ditmarsch, 2005), obtain the Dynamic Epistemic Logic (DEL) I call `$P*$'. It models processes of belief revision in several ways. Original results are presented in the following two sections. The first one of these sections revolves around a formalization of AGM postulates within $P*$ by proving some theorems related to the satisfaction of those postulates by revisions defined in $P*$. The last section features an analysis of $P*$'s revisions that go beyond the mere satisfaction of postulates. I compare their formal behavior with respect to some philosophical criteria. At last, I conclude that the functions presented in (van Ditmarsch, 2005) are not good formalizations of the philosophical intuition behind AGM.
